- **Acceptance criteria:**
  1. Map each of B1–B6 individually to exact repository path and line(s), or mark `UNKNOWN / NOT EVIDENCED`.
  2. Classify each statement as verified repository fact, assumption, or unknown; do not infer absence beyond the inspected repository.
  3. Distinguish hash/recomputation integrity or content consistency from source authenticity and authorization.
  4. Identify attacker capabilities, authoritative identity inputs, and write/replace boundaries only where supported by evidence.
  5. If no independently protected trust/authorization boundary is evidenced, state bounded current-model infeasibility and keep the gate fail-closed; do not claim identity design passes.
- **Deliverable:** B1–B6 evidence map and bounded decision recorded in `INV-DES-03.md` and/or `INV-DES-03-MATRIX-INDEX.md`, with precise citations.
- **Status detail:** B1–B6 evidence map and bounded infeasibility decision are recorded in `INV-DES-03-MATRIX-INDEX.md:23-33`; reviewer acceptance is separate and still pending. TASK-1 deliverable is complete at its bounded static/governance-document scope; no identity gate PASS is implied.
- **Evidence snapshot:** A bounded B1–B6 table was drafted in the formal matrix index based on repository paths/lines inspected. B1: report-gates lists sequence only; direct in-repo calls to checklist_audit were not located by bounded shell/Python searches, which does not establish absence of external/dynamic callers. B2: the repository text describes the required threat model but does not evidence a distinct protected identity authority. B3: snapshot/meta.batch_id and native plan fields exist, but a unique authorized claim/owner/attempt binding independent of the same writable runtime is not evidenced. B4: the checklist reader globs/concatenates matching files and the validator sections by expert_id without batch/hash checks; a trusted authoritative seven-result SHA inventory is not evidenced. B5: prepare writes staged files then performs multiple os.replace calls; interruption/retry/repair invalidation across all paths is not established as an atomic identity protocol; repair baseline is local integrity/structure control only. B6: checklist_audit read errors return nonzero, but report_path is explicitly read and --code has no identity gate; coverage failure propagation to all production consumers is not established. This is repository-bounded evidence; no inference about out-of-repo controls.
- **Bounded decision:** No independently protected, authoritative identity source binding the frozen batch/claim/attempt and seven result digests is evidenced in the reviewed repository. Under the current evidenced permission model, a trustworthy checklist identity gate is not demonstrable; current model is bounded-infeasible absent such authority. Keep stage-A fail-closed; do not calculate coverage from any --code glob hit. This is a design conclusion, not implementation or dynamic PASS. The same bounded conclusion and its citations are recorded in the formal contract `INV-DES-03.md:1003` and matrix index `INV-DES-03-MATRIX-INDEX.md:23-33`.
- **Escalation classification:** YES — a user-level decision is required before any proposal to introduce/procure an independent authoritative identity source or signing/approval mechanism; this ledger records the need only and does not itself request authorization or initiate such work. Rationale: current repository evidence cannot establish trust/authenticity from recomputed hashes alone; changing that would require a new protected authority/signing design and separate permission.

- **Non-goals / prohibited:** Do not run cleanup or induce failures/races; do not inspect runtime/report targets, infer effective root/mode/owner, inspect additional callers, or claim the full cleanup writer/caller set. REM03-01 remains the primary cleanup/deleter mapping.
- **Dependencies:** Parent `INV-DES-03-REM-MAP-READONLY-01`; existing REM03-01 cleanup/deleter cross-reference only.
- **Acceptance criteria:** (1) Record receipt trigger and target selection/path. (2) Distinguish exclusive stage creation from final target replacement; identify explicit versus implicit mode. (3) Map in-slice failure and cleanup propagation, including pre-existing stage/receipt behavior. (4) State positive and negative branches: successful-cleanup plus published marker writes; errors, remaining paths or no marker do not; preserve actual filesystem state, effective permissions and concurrency as UNKNOWN. (5) No executable validation, runtime/DB/data access, or broader caller search.
- **AC/result:** Receipt is attempted only when `errors` and `remaining` are empty. The code enumerates `reports_dir/artifacts/<code>/*/.published.json`, sorts the marker parent paths, and chooses the last; target is that parent directory's `.cleanup.json`. It constructs sibling stage `..cleanup.json.<pid>.next`, opens it using `Path.open("x", encoding="utf-8")` (exclusive creation, no explicit file mode; effective mode depends on process umask and remains unknown), writes JSON plus newline, flushes/fsyncs, then calls `os.replace(stage, target)`; an existing receipt target is not checked and may be replaced. No directory creation or chmod is shown. `result["receipt"]` is set only after replacement. `finally` unlinks the stage with `missing_ok=True`; if exclusive open fails because a same-name stage already exists, the finally block may delete that pre-existing stage. Exceptions from open/write/fsync/replace/unlink are not caught in the cited slice and propagate outward; an unlink error may mask an earlier exception. Actual `reports_dir`/target state, effective mode/owner, concurrent behavior, invocation, outer failure handling and external callers remain UNKNOWN.
- **Verification / deliverable:** Manual source-line and matrix cross-reference review only; no executable validation command is permitted by this START. No script/test/CLI was run; no runtime, DB, report or data was accessed. The first read window also displayed line 111 (blank) due offset alignment; that line was not used, and no other out-of-scope source line/path was read.
- **Risk / rollback:** Existing `.cleanup.json` may be replaced; stage collision/finally-unlink and exception propagation are static failure/cleanup risks, not observed effects. Roll back only these exact authored governance-document changes; no product/source files changed.

- **Status:** RESOLVED BY PM for the original v3 fixture/date gate — that resolution authorized no DB/fixture change or extra build at that time. RUN-02 is a later, separately user-authorized scope governed only by `INV-SMOKE-01-RUN-02.md`; no v4 build is currently started.
- **Owner:** investPM resolved the date selection by preserving the reviewed fixture's `2026-08-01` as-of; this is governance/static analysis only.
- **Evidence:** Frozen fixture mapping specifies `fixed_analysis_date=2026-08-01`; builder's sole market row has `trade_date=20260731`. Chief's proposed `INVEST_ANALYSIS_DATE=2026-10-21` yields market age 82 days, but `shared/data_tools.py:944-948` requires `0 <= age <= 7`; the sole prepare attempt would deterministically fail snapshot validation. No DB modification/rebuild was authorized under the then-current v3 scope; RUN-02 later authorizes only the isolated v4 fixture described in its separate contract.
- **AC / decision:** RESOLVED: new contract records `INVEST_ANALYSIS_DATE=2026-08-01`, matching frozen `fixture-mapping-SYNTHETIC.json` and the reviewed v2 command. The proposed `2026-10-21` date is excluded because it would deterministically fail the <=7-day market-age gate. No fixture/DB rebuild or extra attempt was authorized at that historical v3 checkpoint; RUN-02's separate user authorization is recorded in its own parent task and does not reopen this date-gate decision.
- **Deadline / handoff:** Date blocker resolved by investPM before new contract freeze. Next owner is 投研流程 to deliver v3 manifest bound to the newly frozen contract SHA by 2026-10-21 23:30 +08; then 投研复核 same-SHA review. Until that review passes and PM issues a separate START, keep prepare STOP; no source/DB changes, no `.env` read, no network/Provider.

- **Type / parent / priority:** New standalone governance-document review leaf, derived from the actual `INV-SMOKE-01-RUN-02-PREFLIGHT-01` late-after-STOP/cutoff event (not a synthetic/demo task); linked evidence only, not a child that changes the frozen RUN-02 technical task tree. This exception applies to this task only and does not amend the group's general ACK→PM-START rule or group notice.
- **Owner / roles:** Dispatcher and design author: investPM. Review executor: 投研复核 (independent read-only review). PM acceptance: investPM, limited to this document-review deliverable; it is not engineering QA, implementation approval, or a READY_FOR_CODE decision.

- **Frozen scope / non-goals:** Review only the communication-control text in this section against those existing ledger facts. No source/code or test reads; no command, script, CLI, test, DB, runtime, real-data, Provider, credential or external access; no write except the review receipt in this ledger; no announcement/group-rule changes, actual process operation, preflight rerun, prepare, recovery execution, release or publication. The frozen document candidate is this section's revision 1; do not alter it while review is pending. Any content change requires a new revision and renewed review.
- **Design candidate — required controls:** (1) Every task/attempt records an absolute hard cutoff with timezone, a single executor, the permitted action/write set, and the STOP owner before any start. (2) On an earlier STOP or at the cutoff, work stops; executor acknowledges STOP and reports process state, completion state, output/write set and known side effects. If STOP receipt, process termination, or state cannot be established, record `STOP/UNKNOWN` and do not start or resume. (3) STOP or cutoff immediately invalidates any earlier START for that attempt; a late/old START is rejected, not treated as renewed authority. (4) No automatic retry or recovery. Recovery requires a fresh decision by the authorized decision-maker, a new attempt number, a newly frozen scope/cutoff, and confirmation that the prior process is stopped and its effects are reconciled; unresolved state remains STOP. Existing project and data/provider/QA gates remain unchanged. (5) Manual duplicate check is limited to the original task record and `TASK-ID + revision + attempt + phase`; this is not a lock or concurrency guarantee. Duplicate or uncertain status means STOP, reconcile each attempt, and do not blindly resend.
- **Acceptance criteria:** 投研复核 returns `PASS / FAIL / BLOCKED` on this exact revision and cites the reviewed clauses. PASS requires: (a) all five controls above are explicit and mutually consistent; (b) positive boundary is limited to a frozen, authorized, single attempt before cutoff and before any STOP; (c) negative/failure cases explicitly cover missing authorization, cutoff expiry, STOP, a late stale START, duplicate-start race, and unconfirmed process/status, each failing closed without automatic retry; (d) recovery is possible only through fresh approval/new attempt/reconciled prior state; (e) the text cannot be read as changing global ACK→START, project safety gates, or granting implementation/runtime permission. Any ambiguity, missing case, or unsupported claim is FAIL/BLOCKED with a citation; no status promotion from ACK alone.
- **Verification / evidence:** Manual same-revision text review only, using the exact section and cited PREFLIGHT-01 ledger entries. No executable verification command is permitted under this task. Record the reviewer's ACK, review result, timestamp, exact clauses reviewed and any blockers in this ledger; a review PASS is not implementation or QA evidence.
- **Deadline / escalation:** ACK or refusal/blocker requested by 2026-10-31 18:20 +08; if ACKed, deliver review by 2026-10-31 18:50 +08 so PM can report before Lead's 19:00 deadline. Missing ACK means `WAIT_ACK`, not started; missed review cutoff means STOP/BLOCKED and no automatic extension or retry.

### Manual low-risk document-pilot checklist — v1 (authorized 2026-10-31)

- **Authority / scope:** Lead's latest group instruction expressly authorizes recording and using this checklist for the invest-skill group's low-risk document-task pilot. It does not change the group notice, general ACK→START rule, project gates, or permissions for code/tests/DB/real data/Provider/credentials/external actions/release/destructive work. Pilot ceiling remains five naturally arising new leaf tasks; never create a task to fill the quota. Task Board remains on its native workflow and is not a pilot intake source.
- **Version basis:** This operational checklist is derived from the independently reviewed revision-2 design at lines 948–950 and the bounded review/PM acceptance at lines 972–976. Checklist v1 is ready only for a future eligible natural task; no prospective checklist execution has yet been evidenced.
- **Pre-start GO / NO-GO checklist — complete every item before any START:**
  1. **Natural candidate:** Record the pre-existing trigger and unique TASK-ID; confirm it is a new leaf, genuinely arose from project work, is not a demo, and is not already in flight. If no candidate exists, stop intake; do not invent an ID or split work merely to qualify.
  2. **Scope freeze:** Freeze the exact document-only deliverable, revision, each AC, non-goals, permitted read/write set, single dispatcher, single executor, STOP owner, and any dependency. Scope or AC ambiguity/change returns to design; no execution while unresolved.
  3. **Manual dedup:** Inspect the authoritative task record and accessible current group assignment/status for `TASK-ID + revision + attempt + phase`; record who checked and when. A match in progress, duplicate request, or uncertain state means STOP and reconcile. This key is a ledger lookup aid, not a lock or concurrency guarantee; use one dispatcher and one executor.
  4. **Exact authorization:** Before start, the task record must say `AUTH=GRANTED` with the authorized approver and evidence reference, or `AUTH=NOT_REQUIRED` with the governing policy basis and evidence reference. Evidence must apply to this exact task, scope, revision and attempt. `PENDING`, `REFUSED`, `UNKNOWN`, missing evidence, or mismatch means `WAIT_AUTH / STOP`; ACK never supplies authority. A pilot-level approval is not blanket authorization for a task or excluded action.
  5. **Explicit mode and handshake:** Record one mode. The group default is executor ACK then explicit PM START. `START_ON_ACK` may be used only when this task explicitly invokes the already-approved pilot mode and all authorization/scope/dependency gates above are satisfied. If the mode is absent or unclear, follow the current group default; do not infer an exception.
  6. **Hard cutoff:** Before START, record an absolute deadline including timezone, the specific attempt, and the action permitted before that deadline. No work begins after the cutoff. STOP or cutoff invalidates any earlier START for that attempt; a late START is rejected, not renewed authority.
  7. **STOP evidence:** On STOP/cutoff, stop work and record the STOP notice/acknowledgement, current/completed phase, whether any process remains (or `NOT_APPLICABLE` with reason), outputs/write set and known side effects. If receipt, cessation, or state is unconfirmed, retain `STOP/UNKNOWN`; do not resume.
  8. **Recovery:** No automatic retry/extension. Any recovery needs a fresh explicit authorization for the exact new attempt, a newly frozen scope/AC/cutoff, a fresh assignment, and reconciliation of the prior attempt's process and effects. Unresolved prior state or scope drift remains STOP and returns to design/authority review.
  9. **Closeout / independent check:** Record the exact reviewed/delivered revision, event evidence, `PASS / FAIL / BLOCKED` with citations, PM's bounded acceptance or rejection, and rollback disposition. Independent document review is not engineering QA. Preserve FAIL/STOP evidence; never promote a document PASS into runtime or implementation permission.
- **GO rule:** START is allowed only if all applicable pre-start items are recorded and pass. Any missing/unknown item is a NO-GO and must be stated as `WAIT_ACK`, `WAIT_AUTH`, `STOP`, or `BLOCKED` as applicable; a status post alone is not a gate.

### Pilot record template and actual event example

- **Required fields for each eligible task:** pilot sequence (1–5); natural-origin reason; TASK-ID / revision / attempt / phase; dedup key, checker and result; frozen scope / AC / non-goals / read-write set; `AUTH` state / authority or policy basis / evidence reference; explicit mode; dispatcher / executor / STOP owner; ACK and START evidence; timezone-qualified hard cutoff; state transitions; STOP confirmation / process-state / outputs / side effects; deliverable revision; independent verdict and citations; PM disposition; rollback; attempt/retry count; duration and cost (use `UNKNOWN` or `NOT_MEASURED` where no evidence exists).
- **Actual example — `INV-COMM-PREFLIGHT-STOP-01` (one registered natural remediation leaf; not a synthetic case):** revision 1 review found one blocker because the authorization-before-start condition was missing (lines 939–943). Revision 2 / attempt 2 ACK was not accepted as authorization; the exact per-attempt authorization was absent, so it stopped at the read-only pre-start check as `STOP / WAIT_AUTH`, with no substantive review (lines 953–957). For revision 2 / attempt 3, Lead explicitly authorized the exact bounded read-only review; the record bound `AUTH=GRANTED`, scope and AC, then captured reviewer ACK, PM START, hard cutoff `2026-10-31 19:20 +08`, and independent `PASS` with zero blockers/suggestions and PM's bounded acceptance (lines 959–976). Automatic retries: 0; these were explicit revision/attempt transitions. Exact message timestamps, elapsed duration and cost are not exposed/measured. This is a retrospective record-format example, **not** evidence that checklist v1 was applied prospectively before those events; attempts 1–2 are failure/stop evidence, and attempt 3 is the bounded document-review outcome only.

This commit is a reference base, not a frozen candidate or QA target; the dirty worktree cannot be used for a run. After design review and exact scope release, the snapshot procedure selects a full commit SHA, creates only an explicitly authorized isolated detached checkout, verifies matching HEAD and empty porcelain status, records commit/tree identity and the synthetic-input manifest, and binds every fixture/run/QA receipt to that identity. If code changes are needed, the resulting implementation SHA becomes the candidate. At design-review time no checkout was authorized; the later snapshot authorization and baseline identity recheck are recorded below.
- **Future I/O boundary (design only; exact absolute paths still gated):** `candidate_root` is the selected detached clean checkout and is read-only during a run; `input_root` contains only a frozen synthetic fixture manifest plus synthetic data and annual-report bundle, all read-only; `run_root` is a new, absent, non-overwriting directory under the separately approved runtime root, with naming template `<APPROVED_RUNTIME_ROOT>/INV-SMOKE-03/<candidate_sha>/<attempt>`. Before execution, the authorization must replace placeholders with exact canonical absolute paths, prove `run_root` absent, reject symlinks/escape, and enumerate every allowed output under it. Only `candidate_root` and `input_root` may be read; only `run_root` may be written. No writes to candidate, source repo, fixture inputs, old runtime/worktree or global `/tmp`; no cleanup/retry/publish. DB is `NOT_USED` by default. If a synthetic DB is technically required, its exact new path, read/write/sidecar policy and permission must be separately approved; no production DB. Network, Provider/API, credentials/`.env`, external CLI and real issuer data are denied unless a separate exact authorization is granted; if any are required, STOP rather than use an undocumented fallback.
- **Required manifest structure (schema is design, not generated now):** `schema_version`; `task_id/revision/attempt/phase`; `authorization{AUTH,approver,evidence_ref,scope,hard_cutoff}`; `candidate{repo_id,commit_sha,tree_sha,checkout_path,clean_status,source_manifest_ref}`; `input{fixture_id,synthetic_only,input_manifest_sha256,files[{relative_path,sha256}],db_status}`; `roots{candidate_root,input_root,run_root,run_root_absent_before,containment_result}`; `io_policy{read_roots,write_roots,denied_paths,network,provider,real_data}`; `execution{frozen_command,command_sha256,environment_variable_names,start/end,hard_cutoff}`; `steps[{name,status,exit_code,stdout_sha256,stderr_sha256,outputs}]`; `gates{expert,challenge,cross_review,adjudication,report,manifest}`; `outputs[{relative_path,sha256}]`; `outcome{PASS/FAIL/BLOCKED,retries,duration,cost,stop_state,side_effects,rollback}`; and `independent_qa{owner,candidate_sha,command_ref,verdict,evidence_ref}`. Secret values are never recorded. Exact commands and file lists remain unfrozen until path-specific source design/release.
- **Design acceptance criteria:** (1) Freeze the one-run objective, synthetic-only identity, permitted input/output roots and no-production-publish boundary above. (2) Use the manifest schema above to bind candidate, inputs, task/batch/attempt, exact roots, command/environment, per-step evidence, output hashes and independent QA. (3) Positive case: all mandatory 7-expert outputs and required challenge/cross-review/adjudication/report/manifest gates pass on the same candidate SHA and the final report+manifest verify; no publish. (4) Negative/fault cases: identity/input mismatch, missing required expert result, failed quality gate or untraced content, timeout/STOP, or any out-of-root I/O must fail closed, preserve evidence and produce no accepted report; one attempt only, no automatic retry/cleanup. (5) Preserve current thresholds and safety gates. (6) Exact executable cases/commands require later path-specific source review and release; no source read, checkout, code or test is authorized by this design. (7) A separate qualified engineering-QA owner must independently validate the exact same immutable SHA; document review is not QA, and no `READY_FOR_CODE` or run authorization is implied.
- **Minimal sequence / responsibility:** (1) Current task `INV-SMOKE-03-DESIGN-01` — investPM delivers this source-free design and records its full-ledger SHA. (2) Next, `投研复核` performs a read-only review of this exact section/hash only; the same-scope Lead authorization covers corrections/review without another manual START. PASS is design-document acceptance only, not engineering QA.
